# INVENTORY: everything the original contains, and how much of it is specified

Generated by the investigation's inventory script on 2026-10-27 from the install, the rc00-decoded data and, for the dialog titles that exist only in code, the `S2DNG.exe`. Do not edit by hand: rerun the script.

This file is the measure of fidelity. A row counts as *specified* when the spec search finds its name in a rule, a heading, a constants row or a test row; that is a mention, not proof that every detail of the item is covered. An item no rule names may be covered by a *class rule* (a loader, table or naming scheme stated once for a whole class, declared in the rule as "Covers (inventory): *category* from `path glob`"); its section then reads "(..., class rule)". **Confidence** is the confidence the matched rule states (`unstated` when it gives none). **Verified** is `traced` (named in the Items column of `TRACED.md`: some of its rules measured in the running original), `observed by Jens` (named in `OBSERVED.md`), `file read` or `unused, not read` (the file-open trace of `FILE-TRACE.md`: the original read the item's file, or a rule calls the file unused and the original never read it), `held in memory` or `held under a changed key` (the memory reads of E-trace-007 at trace ticks 1 and 6000: the running original holds the item's text, record or property object as its loader rule predicts), or `none`. "Specs" counts the spec files that mention the item.

Known gaps of the listing itself: menu pages and HUD parts that are built in code without a title appear only through their texts (UI text) and textures (UI texture); models (`.gr2` meshes) appear under the building, settler, animal, ship, item and landscape rows that use them, and any file no row uses is listed under *other file*, so every file under `data/` is accounted for once.
